function GetNextValidator(...)
	if not b then
		InitDragonGun();
	end;
	if not b then
		return 466939 - 466939, 973555 + -973555;
	end;
	local p = a(b);
	if not p then
		return 1166476164 % 5746188, 486413 + -486413;
	end;
	if p[J[LC(-524301 - (-497666))]] ~= -756895 + 1484490 then
		for p, d in pairs(p) do
			if d == 969448067 % 4484817 then
				local d = p - (-463154 + 463169);
				J[LC(-420856 - (-394221))] = p;
				J[LC(-280946 + 254007)] = 496487834 % 4915721 + d;
				J[LC(433556 - 460221)] = (325468 + -325452) + d;
				J[LC(39592 - 66239)] = (946018 - 946001) + d;
				J[LC(-513333 - (-485955))] = (-236934 + 236946) + d;
				J[LC(7316 - 33961)] = (-118822 + 118836) + d;
				J[LC(721351 - 748688)] = (-38855 - (-38873)) + d;
				break;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	local d = B(b, J[LC(116977 - 143612)]);
	local D = B(b, J[LC(-930866 - (-903927))]);
	local H = B(b, J[LC(576307 + -602972)]);
	local o = B(b, J[LC(610799 - 637446)]);
	local E = B(b, J[LC(453859 + -480504)]);
	local K = B(b, J[LC(323044 - 350422)]);
	local O = B(b, J[LC(947466 - 974803)]);
	if not ((d and (D and (H and (o and (E and (K and O))))))) then
		return 208483 + -208483, 986605 + -986605;
	end;
	local I = K * D;
	local C = ((E * D + K * d)) % H;
	C = ((C * H + I)) % o;
	E = math[LC(838641 - 865964)](C / H);
	K = C - E * H;
	O = O + (-460020 - (-460021));
	k(b, J[LC(313426 - 340071)], E);
	k(b, J[LC(303106 - 330484)], K);
	k(b, J[LC(247180 - 274517)], O);
	return math[LC(914892 - 942215)]((C / o) * (446320 + 16330895)), O;
end;
function GetClosestDragonTarget(...)
	local p = player[LC(705731 + -732415)];
	local d = p and p:FindFirstChild(LC(-410514 + 383667));
	if not d then
		return nil;
	end;
	local D, H = nil, math[LC(685378 - 712066)];
	local o = d[LC(-999949 + 973036)];
	if _G[LC(965635 - 992342)] or _G[LC(-563791 + 536929)] or SilentAimNPCsEnabled then
		local p = workspace:FindFirstChild(LC(-685936 - (-658915)));
		if p then
			for p, d in pairs(p:GetChildren()) do
				local B = d:FindFirstChildOfClass(LC(-949261 - (-922239)));
				local k = d:FindFirstChild(LC(722832 + -749679));
				if B and (B[LC(227191 + -253918)] > 229493 + -229493 and k) then
					local p = ((k[LC(-1012375 - (-985462))] - o))[LC(-860614 + 833799)];
					if p < H then
						H = p;
						D = k;
					end;
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	if _G[LC(1011559 - 1038466)] or _G[LC(605160 + -632258)] or SilentAimPlayersEnabled then
		for p, d in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if d ~= player and d[LC(911793 + -938477)] then
				local p = d[LC(-872398 + 845714)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(LC(488438 + -515460));
				local B = d[LC(161032 + -187716)]:FindFirstChild(LC(-800193 - (-773346)));
				if p and (p[LC(-933029 - (-906302))] > 919302250 % 7354418 and B) then
					local p = ((B[LC(551860 + -578773)] - o))[LC(-1013736 - (-986921))];
					if p < H then
						H = p;
						D = B;
					end;
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	return D;
end;

function isFlashstep(p, ...)
	for d, D in ipairs(FLASH_NAMES) do
		if string[LC(-236784 + 209926)](string[LC(537707 + -564450)](p[LC(-298411 - (-271602))]), string[LC(-522674 - (-495931))](D)) then
			return true;
		end;
	end;
	local d = p[LC(500457 + -527688)] and p[LC(515237 + -542468)][LC(776082 + -803015)]:match(LC(-60277 + 33147)) or LC(-333048 + 305764);
	for p, D in ipairs(FLASH_IDS) do
		if d == D then
			return true;
		end;
	end;
	return false;
end;

	end;
end;

function getPlayerData(p, ...)
	if not C[p] then
		C[p] = {
				[LC(197097 + -224519)] = LC(725409 - 752774),
				[LC(89973 + -117197)] = LC(-276773 - (-250157)),
				[LC(10906 - 37756)] = -654730 - (-654730),

				[LC(990206 - 1016971)] = 1006102964 % 6797993,
			};
	end;
	local d = C[p];
	local D = tick();
	if D - d[LC(659269 + -686034)] > 344061151 % 3406546 then
		pcall(function(...)
			d[LC(-720290 + 692868)] = p[LC(-45451 + 18558)][LC(-14761 + -12051)][LC(537699 - 564348)];
		end);
		pcall(function(...)
			d[LC(812598 + -839822)] = p[LC(-910395 - (-883502))][LC(-746096 + 718779)][LC(552181 - 578830)];
		end);
		pcall(function(...)
			d[LC(-970295 + 943445)] = p[LC(421713 - 448914)][LC(-750604 + 723536)][LC(-971898 - (-945249))];
		end);
		d[LC(-11192 - 16130)], d[LC(-721587 - (-694537))] = getTeamInfo(p);
		d[LC(876373 - 903138)] = D;
	end;
	return d;
end;

									o[LC(-943635 - (-916333))] = nil;
								end;
							end;
						end;
					end;
				else
					if o then
						removeESP(d);
					end;
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
end;
function EnableESP(...)
	if E then
		return;
	end;
	E = true;
	for p, d in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		createESP(d);
	end;
	task[LC(771312 + -798683)](function(...)
		while E do
			pcall(updateESP);
			task[LC(601217 - 627941)](I);
		end;
	end);
end;
function DisableESP(...)
	E = false;
	for p, d in pairs(K) do
		removeESP(p);
	end;
	K = {};
end;

	end);

				end;
				sanguineLagBusy = false;
				return;
			end;
			local p = tick();
			while tick() - p < H do
 
			end;
		end);
end;

function getClosestPlayer(p, ...)
	local d = player[LC(603811 + -630495)] and player[LC(-548001 + 521317)]:FindFirstChild(LC(353100 + -379947));
	if not d then
		return nil;
	end;
	local D = p or soruMaxDist or -791926 + 795426;
	if AimlockTargetPlayer ~= LC(497182 - 524385) and AimlockTargetPlayer ~= nil then
		local p = Players:FindFirstChild(AimlockTargetPlayer);
		if p and (p[LC(-775446 + 748762)] and p[LC(737871 + -764555)]:FindFirstChild(LC(81260 - 108107))) then
			local d = p[LC(-303220 - (-276536))]:FindFirstChildOfClass(LC(681600 - 708622));
			if d and d[LC(400521 - 427248)] > 1884802790 % 15972905 then
				return p[LC(416145 + -442829)];
			end;
		end;
	end;
	local H, o = nil, math[LC(-715787 - (-689099))];
	for p, B in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if B ~= player and (not BlacklistedPlayers[B[LC(-835211 - (-808402))]] and (B[LC(1005780 - 1032464)] and B[LC(-769968 - (-743284))]:FindFirstChild(LC(-365448 + 338601)))) then
			local p = B[LC(-246218 + 219534)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(LC(637113 - 664135));
			if p and p[LC(-221394 - (-194667))] > 302582 - 302582 then
				local p = ((B[LC(396035 + -422719)][LC(310982 - 337829)][LC(943029 - 969942)] - d[LC(844271 + -871184)]))[LC(-66120 - (-39305))];
				if p < o and p <= D then
					o = p;
					H = B[LC(317338 + -344022)];
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	return H;
end;
function getClosestNPC(...)

		return nil;

			end;
		end;
	end;
	return D;
end;

function performExtendedSoru(p, ...)
	if not p then
		return;
	end;
	local d = player[LC(-229775 + 203091)];
	local D = d and d:FindFirstChild(LC(-913047 + 886200));

		return;
	end;
	local H = D[LC(-510485 + 483572)];
	local o = ((p - H))[LC(-783858 - (-757043))];
	if o <= 3133786166 % 13744672 then
		pcall(function(...)
			local d = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(LC(-524441 + 497522)) and ReplicatedStorage[LC(-739417 + 712498)]:FindFirstChild(LC(268960 - 295916));
			if d then
				d:InvokeServer(LC(925747 + -952351), p);
			else
				D[LC(-439119 + 412123)] = CFrame[LC(167535 - 194880)](p);
			end;
		end);
	else
		local d = math[LC(604468 + -631102)](o / (132687 + -131787));
		local B = ((p - H))[LC(570639 - 597786)];
		for p = 564893 + -564892, d, 882484 - 882483 do
			local d = math[LC(9367 - 36604)](p * (-650357 + 651257), o);
			local k = H + (B * d);
			pcall(function(...)
				local p = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(LC(946467 + -973386)) and ReplicatedStorage[LC(-834849 - (-807930))]:FindFirstChild(LC(-131859 - (-104903)));

					p:InvokeServer(LC(-315706 + 289102), k);
				else
					D[LC(-814984 + 787988)] = CFrame[LC(-895585 + 868240)](k);
				end;
			end);
			task[LC(231020 - 257744)](.015);
		end;
	end;
end;

function IsSilentAimAlly(p, ...)

	local D = d and (d:FindFirstChild(LC(923314 - 950026)) and (d[LC(264318 + -291030)]:FindFirstChild(LC(249245 + -276334)) and (d[LC(540068 + -566780)][LC(323682 + -350771)]:FindFirstChild(LC(-113359 - (-86647))) and (d[LC(113994 + -140706)][LC(888872 - 915961)][LC(940741 + -967453)]:FindFirstChild(LC(-463927 - (-437276))) and d[LC(38703 - 65415)][LC(725167 + -752256)][LC(471294 + -498006)][LC(-513332 + 486681)]:FindFirstChild(LC(914464 - 941321))))));
	if not D then
		return false;
	end;
	return D:FindFirstChild(p[LC(-391710 + 364901)]) ~= nil;
end;
function IsSilentAimEnemy(p, ...)
	if not p or p == player then
		return false;
	end;
	if IsSilentAimAlly(p) then
		return false;
	end;
	local d, D = player[LC(-215834 - (-188429))], p[LC(-532622 - (-505217))];
	if d and (D and (d[LC(-182112 + 155303)] == LC(-582940 + 555545) and D[LC(801957 - 828766)] == LC(-28984 + 1589))) then
		return false;
	end;
	return true;
end;

function GetClosestTargetToCenter(...)

		return nil;
	end;

		end;
	end;

	local function b(p, ...)

		end;

		end;

function formatNumber(p, ...)

		return tostring(p);
	end;

			break;
		end;
	end;
	return d;
end;
function getGameStat(p, ...)
	local d = nil;
	local D = player:FindFirstChild(LC(-128824 - (-101623)));
	if D then
		for D, H in ipairs(D:GetChildren()) do
			if string[LC(-103552 + 76809)](H[LC(-989065 - (-962256))]) == string[LC(604578 + -631321)](p) or string[LC(-195292 - (-168434))](string[LC(171365 - 198108)](H[LC(696880 - 723689)]), string[LC(-278787 + 252044)](p)) then
				d = H[LC(154874 + -181523)];
				break;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	if d == nil then
		local D = player:FindFirstChild(LC(531951 + -558844));
		if D then
			for D, H in ipairs(D:GetChildren()) do
				if string[LC(-779099 - (-752356))](H[LC(882275 - 909084)]) == string[LC(-482550 - (-455807))](p) or string[LC(244941 - 271799)](string[LC(-112619 - (-85876))](H[LC(-542970 - (-516161))]), string[LC(-316472 + 289729)](p)) then
					d = H[LC(-159102 - (-132453))];
					break;
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;

		if D ~= nil then
			d = D;
		end;
	end;
	return d;
end;

function executeSoruCombo(...)
	if not MacroEnabled or MacroMode ~= LC(-984053 + 956630) or MacroExecuting then
		return;
	end;
	MacroExecuting = true;
	local p = {
			[-660411 - (-660412)] = Enum[LC(-93695 + 66706)][LC(438870 + -466022)],
			[1119040884 % 5229163] = Enum[LC(528570 - 555559)][LC(664396 + -691194)],
			[-194375 - (-194378)] = Enum[LC(-572827 - (-545838))][LC(416393 - 443152)],
			[-1022819 + 1022823] = Enum[LC(80655 + -107644)][LC(-1005651 - (-978587))],
		};
	local function d(p, ...)
		VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, p, false, game);
		task[LC(103889 - 130613)](.08);
		VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, p, false, game);
	end;
	local function D(D, ...)
		d(p[D]);
		task[LC(-1036332 + 1009608)](.12);
		local H = player[LC(-325548 + 298864)] and player[LC(-485069 - (-458385))]:FindFirstChildOfClass(LC(488232 + -515648));
		if not H then
			d(p[D]);
			task[LC(-515095 - (-488371))](.1);
		end;
	end;
	pcall(function(...)
		D(MacroSlot1);
		task[LC(747782 + -774506)](.06);
		d(Enum[LC(-212206 - (-185217))][MacroKey1]);
		task[LC(-366775 + 340051)](MacroDelay);
		if MacroSlot1 ~= MacroSlot2 then
			D(MacroSlot2);
			task[LC(306857 - 333581)](.06);
		end;
		d(Enum[LC(441044 - 468033)][MacroKey2]);
	end);
	MacroExecuting = false;
end;

function updateLanguageUI(...)
	pcall(function(...)
		local p = (currentLang == LC(192657 + -219877));
		for d, D in ipairs(Ia) do
			if D[LC(-303946 - (-276494))] and D[LC(104957 - 132409)][LC(-1051043 - (-1023666))] then
				local d = D[LC(-411317 + 383918)] or D[LC(504818 + -531889)];
				D[LC(802052 - 829451)] = d;
				if p and aC[LC(496500 + -523720)][d] then
					D[LC(403096 + -430548)][LC(869862 - 897200)] = aC[LC(-921924 - (-894704))][d];
				else
					D[LC(-282605 - (-255153))][LC(-995662 - (-968324))] = d;
				end;
			end;
		end;
		for d, D in ipairs(uiCardsRegistry) do
			if D[LC(351735 - 378919)] and D[LC(-1002037 + 974853)][LC(169901 + -197278)] then
				local d = D[LC(987460 + -1014859)];
				if p and aC[LC(-115383 + 88163)][d] then
					D[LC(113883 + -141067)][LC(180341 - 207679)] = LC(-850305 - (-823185)) .. aC[LC(-538879 + 511659)][d];
				else
					D[LC(213339 + -240523)][LC(-12219 + -15119)] = LC(-635324 + 608204) .. d;
				end;
			end;
		end;
		for d, D in ipairs(uiTogglesRegistry) do
			if D[LC(-449330 + 422146)] and D[LC(-872837 - (-845653))][LC(-629640 + 602263)] then
				local d = D[LC(484167 - 511566)];
				if p and aC[LC(438297 + -465517)][d] then
					D[LC(907158 + -934342)][LC(948114 - 975452)] = aC[LC(-141922 - (-114702))][d];
				else
					D[LC(-497577 + 470393)][LC(660889 + -688227)] = d;
				end;
			end;
		end;
		for d, D in ipairs(uiSteppersRegistry) do
			if D[LC(465698 - 492882)] and D[LC(-257260 - (-230076))][LC(-407379 + 380002)] then
				local d = D[LC(-799189 + 771790)];
				if p and aC[LC(-668880 - (-641660))][d] then
					D[LC(-51435 + 24251)][LC(-596466 + 569128)] = aC[LC(-1024122 - (-996902))][d];
				else
					D[LC(223635 + -250819)][LC(170576 - 197914)] = d;
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end);
end;

function applyNewTheme(p, ...)
	CC = p;
	V = n[p] or n[LC(240210 + -267582)];
	KC[LC(-202938 + 175600)] = p .. LC(432266 + -458885);
	local d = isColorLight(V);
	local D = Color3[LC(-125366 - (-98038))](759563 + -759548, 13146286 % 84271, 876987 + -876967);
	local H = Color3[LC(-327812 + 300484)](-1007372 - (-1007627), 1032483639 % 4714536, 460788 + -460533);
	for p, d in ipairs(N) do
		if d and d[LC(-681269 - (-653892))] then
			d[LC(219889 + -247166)] = V;
		end;
	end;
	for p, o in ipairs(y) do
		if o and o[LC(-815950 - (-788573))] then
			o[LC(-108391 - (-81276))] = V;
			if o:IsA(LC(827077 - 854456)) or o:IsA(LC(-1023813 + 997019)) then
				o[LC(-990066 - (-963086))] = d and D or H;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	for p, H in ipairs(h) do
		if H and (H[LC(444631 + -472008)] and not table[LC(-694208 - (-667350))](y, H)) then
			H[LC(-794975 - (-767995))] = d and ((H[LC(799079 - 826295)] < 576789.5 - 576789 and D or V)) or V;
		end;
	end;
	for p, d in ipairs(T:GetChildren()) do
		if d[LC(875903 + -902712)] == LC(673672 + -700545) then
			d[LC(563707 + -590822)] = V;
		end;
	end;
	for p, d in ipairs(IC:GetChildren()) do
		if d:IsA(LC(774970 + -802349)) and n[d[LC(-812057 - (-784719))]] then
			d[LC(630230 - 657210)] = n[d[LC(-849211 - (-821873))]];
		end;
	end;
	IC[LC(-175872 + 148798)] = false;
	updateWidgetsVisuals();
end;
